# GasGas MC 125 2024: aftermarket parts

4509 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 393 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 665 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3451 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
